🤖 RAG ANSWER (47.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus Internship Program, you must meet the following
   criteria: have a minimum GPA of 2.20 for undergraduates or 2.5 for
   graduate students, and obtain an acceptance letter from a host
   organization for a minimum internship duration of 2 months. Applications
   are submitted through the Online Application System, where you must upload
   your CV, transcript, and acceptance letter. Applications are evaluated
   based on Erasmus+ scores, and the Internship Office announces the list of
   selected students by the end of April. Before starting the internship, you
   must have the Erasmus+ Internship Mobility Agreement signed by the host
   organization and complete the Learning Agreement, Grant Agreement, and
   Travel and Insurance documents.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (69.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe hesaplaması şu şekilde yapılır:   1)
   **Dil puanı (%50) ve GNO (%50) bileşeniyle hesaplanır**: Önce
   TOEFL/YDS/ELAE gibi sınav sonuçları Yükseköğretim Kurulunun eşdeğerlilik
   tablosuna göre dil puanına dönüştürülür. Bu puanın %50'si ve GNO'nun
   %50'si alınarak Erasmus+ Puanı oluşturulur.   2) **Hibe miktarı Ulusal
   Ajans tarafından belirlenir**: Ülke bazında aylık hibe tutarları her yıl
   Ulusal Ajans tarafından duyurulur (örneğin, Mart ayında planlama yapılır,
   Nisan sonunda kontenjanlar bildirilir).   3) **Günlük hesaplama**:
   Tamamlanmayan aylar için hibe, kalan gün sayısı × (aylık hibe/30)
   formülüyle belirlenir. Ödemeler, Ulusal Ajans'ın fon aktarımını takiben
   yurt dışına çıkış öncesi veya dönem başında yapılır.   Lisans ve
   lisansüstü öğrenciler için hesaplama yöntemi aynıdır, ancak staj
   hareketliliğinde asgari süre 2 aydır (öğrenimde 3 ay veya 1 akademik
   dönem).
------------------------------------------------------------

[Learning Agreement Process]

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (44.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) process for exchange students involves several
   steps. The student prepares the LA, detailing the courses to be taken
   abroad, and submits it within the period announced by the International
   Relations Office (IRO). The LA must be approved by the exchange academic
   advisor, who determines and approves course selections, SU equivalent
   credits, course types, and ECTS credit values. The student must ensure the
   LA is signed by the partner institution and inform the advisor of any
   course changes before completing the term abroad. After signing, the
   student submits the approved LA to the IRO, which forwards it to the
   Faculty Administrative Officer for final approval. The LA ensures that
   courses taken abroad are recognized in place of SU courses and aligns with
   SU graduation requirements.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (48.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Students can object to disciplinary sanctions imposed by discipline
   supervisors or committees within **15 days** by submitting a written
   appeal to the University Administrative Board. The board examines and
   decides on the objection within **15 days**, either accepting or rejecting
   the sanction. If rejected, the disciplinary board or supervisor finalizes
   the objection, considering the grounds for rejection. For dormitory-
   related penalties, objections to removal must be submitted to the
   Provincial Directorate within **5 working days**, with a decision made
   within **7 working days**. After the objection process, students can still
   appeal to administrative jurisdiction.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (101.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To open a graduate program, the Graduate School Board submits a proposal
   with required information (courses, credits, purpose) to YÖK, after
   obtaining UEC's opinion and VP-E's coordination, followed by Academic
   Council approval and Board of Trustees approval. The program begins after
   YÖK approval.  For closing a graduate program, the Graduate School Board
   proposes closure with justifications, obtains UEC opinion and VP-E
   coordination, then submits to the Academic Council and Board of Trustees
   for approval. The decision is communicated to YÖK, and program details are
   archived within 1 week of YÖK approval.  Changes to existing graduate
   programs (courses, credits, codes) follow separate procedures for course
   modifications.
------------------------------------------------------------

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

================
[Generation] Stripped document codes from answer.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (58.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   At Sabancı University, GPA is calculated by multiplying the SU credit of
   each course by its coefficient and then dividing the total credit points
   by the total SU credits of all courses taken in that term (for Graduate
   students) or all courses taken since enrollment (for Undergraduate
   students).   For Undergraduate students, the last grade for a repeated
   course is factored into the GPA calculation, and all decimals are
   considered for academic ranking.   For Graduate students, the same method
   applies but focuses on courses taken in the related term, with all
   decimals included in calculations. Both GPAs are displayed with two
   decimal places.
------------------------------------------------------------

[Generation] Stripped document codes from answer.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (44.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   For undergraduate students, leave of absence requests must be submitted by
   the last business day of the 7th week following the first day of classes
   in the academic year. Accepted requests require payment of 50% of the
   semester fee for a semester leave or 50% of the annual tuition for a full
   academic year leave.   Graduate students must apply with a petition to the
   Graduate School Directorate if they wish to return early from leave, and
   any leave fee paid will be deducted from the tuition of the registered
   semester upon approval.   Leave can be granted for health, financial,
   family, personal, academic reasons, or unexpected situations, and must be
   documented. Special cases like conscription or arrest allow for
   retroactive leave if applied within 2 months.   For faculty and staff,
   unpaid leave requires one semester or one month advance notice,
   respectively, and must specify exact dates. Unpaid leave cannot exceed one
   year and requires arrangements to ensure duties are covered. Employees
   absent beyond 3 business days without notice lose employment rights.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (42.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include:  1.
   **Warning** and **reprimand** (for minor offenses).  2. **Suspension from
   the university** (ranging from 1 week to 1 month or 1-2 semesters).  3.
   **Dismissal** (expulsion) from the university (for severe or repeated
   offenses).  4. **Removal from dormitories** (for violations of housing
   rules).   Repeat offenses result in a penalty one grade heavier, but
   dismissal cannot be imposed for repetition alone. Suspended students are
   barred from campus and must pay tuition for suspended semesters. Warnings
   and reprimands may be appealed to the Disciplinary Board.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (47.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To initiate the graduation audit process, students must apply through the
   SIS within the dates specified in the academic calendar. The SR and
   Faculties/Institutes then review and confirm eligibility based on
   completed courses, credits, GPA, and thesis requirements (for graduate
   programs). If a student doesn't qualify, they must reapply the next
   semester. Once approved, diplomas are issued in Turkish with an English
   translation, signed by relevant authorities, and delivered personally, via
   authorized proxy, or by registered mail. Students and Institutes monitor
   academic progress and thesis milestones every semester to ensure
   eligibility. Disciplinary suspensions or leave periods disqualify students
   from being graduation candidates.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   If you find a lost item on Sabancı University campus, you should report it
   by calling extension 3565 or bring it to the Security Office in Building
   D2. Security officers will prepare a two-copy Found Item Return Form,
   which both you and the officer will sign. The original form stays with the
   Security Unit, while you receive the second copy. Found items are then
   numbered and categorized as valuable or non-valuable, placed in separate
   labeled bags, and stored for up to one year. If you lose an item on
   campus, you must notify the Security Unit verbally and in writing, and if
   the loss occurs off-campus, you must report it to the police or
   gendarmerie and obtain an official report.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (48.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services must be requested by filling out the
   Special Event and Catering Request Form on SUForm at least 30 days before
   the event. The Food and Beverage Services (FBS) unit evaluates the
   request, provides a preliminary offer, and prepares the menu and
   specifications. The requesting unit then initiates a procurement request
   (PR) based on the approved offer. The Procurement and Operations Unit
   (POP) selects a suitable company, and FBS coordinates with the company to
   finalize event details and ensure all necessary measures are in place. All
   communication with suppliers must go through FBS, not directly.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Sabancı Üniversitesi'nde kampüs servis hizmetleri şu şekilde düzenlenir:
   1. **Vardiya ve Toplama Servisleri:** Ulaşım Hizmetleri (UH) tarafından
   belirlenen saat ve güzergâhlara göre vardiyalı çalışanların ulaşımı
   sağlanır.  2. **Shuttle Servisleri:** Kampüs-Şehir arasında belirlenen
   güzergah ve saatlerde hizmet verilir.  3. **Özel Öğrenci Servisleri:**
   Öğrenci ile servis firması arasındaki koordinasyon sağlanır, ancak
   sorumluluk Yüklenici firmaya aittir.  4. **Kampüs İçi Ring Servisi:** UH
   tarafından belirlenen saat ve güzergahta Ana Kapı'dan başlayarak Spor
   Merkezi, Gösteri Merkezi, DO, YBF Önü, Yurtlar, SUNUM ve tekrar Ana
   Kapı'ya dönen bir ring hattı işler (hafta içi ve hafta sonu farklı
   saatlerde).  5. **Özel Servis Talepleri:** Üniversite birimleri tarafından
   akademik, sosyal vb. etkinlikler için ücret karşılığında özel servis talep
   edilebilir.  6. **Servis Kuralları:** Araç hareket halindeyken koltuktan
   kalkılmaz, emniyet kemeri açılmaz, hayvan taşınmaz ve sürücünün dikkati
   dağıtılmaz.
